           │     def types(self):                                                                                                       │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the type declarations.                                                                       │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines a 8-bit type called T:                                           │
           │         ```                                                                                                                │

           │             self.T = BitVector(8)                                                                                          │
           │         ```                                                                                                                │
           │         """                                                                                                                │
           │         pass                                                                                                               │
           │                                                                                                                            │
           │     def locals(self):                                                                                                      │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the local variables and their types.                                                         │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an 8-bit variable x                                              │
           │         and an integer variable y:                                                                                         │
           │         ```                                                                                                                │
           │         def locals(self):                                                                                                  │
           │             self.x = BitVector(8)                                                                                          │
           │             self.y = Integer()                                                                                             │
           │         ```                                                                                                                │
           │         """                                                                                                                │
           │         pass                                                                                                               │
           │                                                                                                                            │
           │     def inputs(self):                                                                                                      │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the input variables and their types.                                                         │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an input variable x,                                             │
           │         which is an array of 8-bit bitvectors indexed by 2-bit bitvectors:                                                 │
           │         ```                                                                                                                │
           │         def inputs(self):                                                                                                  │
           │             self.x = Array(BitVector(2), BitVector(8))                                                                     │
           │         ```                                                                                                                │
           │         """                                                                                                                │
           │         pass                                                                                                               │
           │                                                                                                                            │
           │     def outputs(self):                                                                                                     │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the output variables and their types.                                                        │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an output variable y,                                            │
           │         which is a real number:                                                                                            │
           │         ```                                                                                                                │
           │         def outputs(self):                                                                                                 │
           │             self.y = Real()                                                                                                │

           │         """                                                                                                                │
           │         pass                                                                                                               │

           │         ```                                                                                                                │

           │         ```                                                                                                                │
           │         """                                                                                                                │
           │         pass                                                                                                               │

           │         variable self.c:                                                                                                   │
           │         ```                                                                                                                │

           │         ```                                                                                                                │
           │         """                                                                                                                │
           │         pass                                                                                                               │
           │                                                                                                                            │
           │     def init(self):                                                                                                        │
           │         """(Optional) Defines how variables are initialized.                                                               │
           │         For example, the following implementation initializes x to 0 if y is                                               │
           │         greater than or equal to 20:                                                                                       │
           │         ```                                                                                                                │

           │             if self.y >= 20:                                                                                               │

           │         ```                                                                                                                │
           │         """                                                                                                                │

           │         self.pop = Boolean                                                                                                 │

           │         if self.pop and (self.top > 0):                                                                                    │
           │             if self.push and (self.top < self.n):                                                                          │
           │                 if self.push:                                                                                              │

           │ block: init                                                                                                                │
           │ fix: The initialization phase does not set up the initial state of the stack 's'. It should initialize 's' as an array     │
           │ with 'n' elements all set to False (representing an empty stack).                                                          │
           │                                                                                                                            │
           │ block: next                                                                                                                │
           │ fix: The logic for stack operations transitions is incorrect and should be restructured. The `if self.push` inside the     │
           │ push condition is redundant and does not handle the pop condition correctly. They should be in separate conditional checks │
           │ for independent operations between push and top handling separately when push is allowed and top is less than n as well as │
           │ when pop is initiated.                                                                                                     │
           │                                                                                                                            │
           │ block: specification                                                                                                       │
           │ fix: The invariants 'integer_inv', 'i_inv', and 'int_inv' are misleading and incorrect. They should be focusing on integer │
           │ handling within 's', ensuring the condition checks on stack integrity during operations are based on proper transitions    │
           │ and setup in 'init'. Remove unnecessary invariants and ensure stack properties are maintained for pop and push operations  │
           │ only.                                                                                                                      │
